(iv) Should be a qualified legal practitioner i.e.

New Delhi, the I4th February, 1990 

G.S.R. 167.—-In exercise of the powers conferred by 
rule 1 of order XXVII of the First Schedule 10 the Code 
of CiviJ Procedure 1908 (5 of 1908) and in .supersession of 
the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry 
of Law No. SRO 351 dated 2.1-1-1958 and 165 dated the 1st 
■September, 1953, the Central Government hereby appoints:—■ 

(i) The officers specified in the schedule annexed hereto 
as persons bv whom plaints and wTitten statements in suits 
in imy court of civil jurisdiction or (in writ proceed'ns) by 
or against the Central Government shall be signed; 

(ii) Those of the officers referred to in sub-clause (i) 
who are acquainted with the facts of the case, as persons 
by whom such plaints and written statements shall be 
verified.

MINISTRY OF HOME AFFAIRS 
New Delhi, the 5th March, 1990 

G S.R. 169.—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution nnd in supersession 
of the National Police Academy (Gazetted Staff) Rales, 1971, 
except a.s respecN things dune or omitted to done before 
such supersession, the President hereby makes the following 
rules regulating the method of recruitment to tfie post of 
Hindi Instructor in the Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel National 
Police Academy, Hyderabad, namely :— 

1. Short title and commenccment.~(l) These rules may be 
called the Sardar Vallabhbhai Palcl National Police Academy 
(Hindi Instructor) Recruitment Rules, 1990. 

(21 They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and scale of pay.—The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scale of 
pay attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 
of the Schedule, annexed to thc.se rules. 

3 Method of recruitment, age limit, qualification, etc.— 
The method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, qua¬ 
lifications and other matters relating thereto shall bo as spe¬ 
cified in columns 5 to 14 of the said Schedule. 


4. Disqualification.—No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person, 

• * 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law appllacble 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and that 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax.—Where the Central C^ovemment is of 
the opinion that It Is necessary or expedient so to do, it 
may, by order, for reasons to be recorded In writing, and 
in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, 
relax any of the provisions of these rules in respect of any 
class or category of persons. 

6. Saving.—Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to bei 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes, fhe 
Ex-Servicemen and other special categories of persons in ac¬ 
cordance wifh the orders issued by the Central Government 
from time to time in this regard.

oth.rwise w.ll qualified.

ministry of finance 

(Department of Economic Alfaiis) 

New Delhi, the 20th Ecbriiary, 1990 

GSR 132—In exercise of the powers confcircd by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 


hereby makes the tolluwing rules to amend the India Govern¬ 
ment Mint, Calcutta (Group ‘C posts) Recruitment Rules, 
1986, namely :— 

1. (1) These rules may he called (he India Government 
Mint, Calcutta (Group ‘C‘ posts) Recruitment (AmnedmentJ 
Rules, 1989. 



540 THE gazette OF INDIA : MARCH 24 , 1990 /CHAITRA 3, 1912 [Part ll—S ec. 3(i)] 


“Transfer on deputation.—Officers under the Central 
Government holding analogous posts or officers 
with at least 8 years regular service in the scale of 
pay of Rs. 950—1500 or equivalent period of 
deputation including period of deputation in an¬ 
other ex-cadre post held immediately pieccding 
this appointment m the same organisation/depart- 
ment shall ordinarily not exceed 3 years. 


(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the India Government Mint, Calcutta 
(Group ‘C’ posts) Recruitment Rules, 1986, against serial 
number 9 relating to the post of Upper Division Clerk,— 

(i) In column 12, for the existing entries, the following 

shall be substituted, namely :— 

“By promotion failing which by transfer on deputation 
find failing both by direct recruitment.” 

(ii) In column 13, for the existing entries, the following 

entries shall be substituted, namely;—■ 

“Promotion.—Lower Division Clerks in the pay scale 
of Rs. 260—400 with 8 years regular service in 
the grade. 


(File No. 5/9/87-Coin.I] 

G. S, GREWAL, Special Officer (C&Cj

tion G.S.R, No. 7 dated 3-1-1987 in the Gazette 
of India, Part II, Section (3)(i) at page 12 to

THE GAZ ETTE OF INDIA : MARCH 24, 1990/CHAITRA 3, 1912 [Part H— Sec. 3(i)] 

(Department of Revenue) 

New Delhi, the 1st March, 199(> 


G.S.R. 17J,—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 ot the Constitution and in supersession 
of the Income-tax Department Non-Gazetted Ministerial Posts 
Recruitment Rules, 1969, except as respects things done or 
omitted to be done before such supersession, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the posts ip the Income-tax Department, 
namely:— 

1, Short title and commencement,—(1) These rules may be 
called Income-tax Department (Group ‘C’) Recruitment Rules, 

(2j They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette, 

(3; Application.—'Ihese rules shall apply to the post spe¬ 
cified in Col, 1 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

(4j Number of posts, classification and scale of pay.—The 
number of the said posts, its classification and the scales of 
pay attaclicd thereto shall be as specified in columns (2) to 
(4j of the said Schedule. 

(5) Method of recruitment, age limit and qualifications 
etc.—The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters connected therewith shall he as specified 
in columns (5J to (14) of the Schedule aforesaid : 

Provided that;— 

(i) Recruitment by promotion against posts in an Estab¬ 

lishment Unit shall be made only from amongst per¬ 
sons beloning to the Cadres of that particular unit. 

(ii) Nothing in these lules shall aifect reservations, relaxa¬ 

tion of age limit and other concessions icquired to 
be provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled 
Tribes, Ex-servicemen and other special categories 
of persons, in accordance with the orders issued by 
the Central Government from lime to time in this 
regard. 

(iii) The Central Board of Direct Taxes may, if it consi¬ 

ders to be expedient or necessary in the public inte¬ 
rest or on compassionate grounds as to do, and 


subject to such conditions as it may specify having 
regard to the circumsiances of the ca.se and for 
reasons to bo recorded in writing, permit a post in 
one Establishment Unit to filled by transfer to that 
Unit of a person belonging to the cadre of another 
Unit. 

(iv) The Central Board of Direct Taxes may permit ap¬ 
pointment of meritorious sportsmen/sportswomen in 
accordance with the general orders issued by Central 
Government from time to lime in relaxation of the 
prescribed educational qualification for a limited 
period of 2 years within which they shall have to 
acquire the prescribed qualihcaiion, failing which 
the services of such a sportsman/sportswomag shall 
be liable to be terminated forthwith 

(6) Disqualificaiions.—No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living; or 

(b) who, having a spouse living has entered into or con¬ 
tracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be liable for appointment to any of the said posts : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applic¬ 
able to such person and the other party to the marriage jmd 
that tlicre are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
trom the operation of this rule. 

7. Researvation of vacancies for State Government emp¬ 
loyees.—Nolwithstuncling anything contained in these rules, 
one-third of the total number of permanent vacancies occur¬ 
ring in a calendar year in each of the categories of posts 
specified in the Schedule to be filled by direct recruitment 
shall be filled up by transfer from among the employee? of 
the State Government concerned, in accordance with Jhc 
general instructions issued by the Central Government from 
time to time. 

8. Power to relax.—Where ihe Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any 
of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or 
category of persons.

MINISTRY OF HUMAN RESOURCE DEVELOPMENT 
(Department of Education) 

New Delhi, the 1st March, 1990 

G-S.R. 174,—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following Rules regulating the method of recruit¬ 
ment of the jxwt of Research Assistant in the Department of 
Education, Union Territory of Lakshadweep, namely:— 

1. Short title and commencement.—(1) These rules may be 
called tho Lakshadweep Administration, Research Assistant 
Recruitment Rules, 1989. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and scale of pay.—^The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scale or 
pay attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 
of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications, 
etc.—The method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, 
qualifications and other matters relating thereto shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 14 of the said Schedule, 


4. Disqualificatioois.—No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living; or 

(b) who, Eaving a spouse living, has entered into or con¬ 

tracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for the appointment to the said post; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marraige is permissible under the personal law ap¬ 
plicable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any 
person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax.—^Wherc the Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in con¬ 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax 
■any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class 
or category of persons, 

6 . Saving,—Nothing in these rules shall effect reservation, 
relaxation regarding age limit and other concessions requlreii 
to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Schdeuled Tribes, 
Ex-scrvicemen and other special categories of persons in ac¬ 
cordance with the orders issued by the Central GovernmeJT 
from time to time in this regard.

of one year and having the requisite experi¬ 
ence and qualification prescribed for 
Officers on transfer on deputation shall 
also be considered. Such persons could 
be given deputation terms upto the date 
on which they arc duo for release from 
Armed Forces; thereafter they may bo 
atnlinued on re-employment (Date of 
superannuation with reference to civil 
posts). 


Education Officer -^Member 

Principal —^Member 

Note ; The proceedings of the Departmental 
Promotion Committee relating to Confii- 
mation of a direct recruit shall be sent to the 
Commission for approval. If however, these 
are not approved by the Commission a 
fresh meeting of the Departmental Promo¬ 
tion Committee to be presided over by the 
Chairman or a Member of the Union Public 
Service Commission shall be held.

C.S.R, 175.—Til exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
mates the following rules icgulating the method of recruit¬ 
ment of the post of Subject Experts in the Department of 
Education, Union Territory of Lakshadweep, namely:— 

1. Sliort title and commencement.—(1) These rules may be 
called the Lakshadweep Administration, Subject Experts Re¬ 
cruitment Rules, 1989, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and scale of pay.—^The 
number of the said post, its classifications and (he scale of 
pay attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 
of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment/age limit and other qiialificatiofis, 

etc,_^Tbe method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, 

qualifications and other matters relating thereto shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 14 of the said Schedule. 


4. Disqualifications.—^No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any 
person froni the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax.—^Wliere the Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to bo recorded in writing and in con¬ 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission, fdlax 
any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any 
class or category of persons. 

6 . Saving,—Nothing in these rules shall afilect reservation, 
relaxation regarding age limit and other concessions required 
to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes, 
(Jic Ex-servicemen and other special categories of persons in 
accordance with the orders issued by the Central GovernmeBt 
from time to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF INDUSTRY 
(Depurtmint of Chemicals & Petrochemicals) 

Office of the Development Commissioner (PI) 

New Delhi, the 14lh December, 1989 

O.S.R. 176.—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution and in supertession 
of the Ministry of Chemicals and Fertilizers. Office of the 
Development Commissioner (Drugs), Senior Investigator Re¬ 
cruitment Rules, 1984, except as respects things done or 
omitted to be done before such supersession, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the recruitment 
to the posts of Senior Investigator in the Ministry of Industry, 
Department of Chemicals and Petrochemicals, Office of the 
Development Commissioner (Pharmaceuticals Industry), 
namely ;— 

1. Short title and commencement,—(1) These rules may be 
called the Ministry of Industry, Department of Chemicals 
and Petrochemicals, Office of tho Development Commissioner 
(Pharmaceutical Industry), Senior Investigator Recruitment 
Rules, 198SF, 

(2) It shall come Into force on tho date of their publica¬ 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification, scale of pay.—The number of 
posts, classiflcatloE and the scales of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said Schedule. 


3. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications, etc.— 
The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other 
matters relating thereto shall be as specified in columns 5 
to 13 of the said Schedule, 

4. Disqualifications.—No perscM,-- 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person; 

shall be eligible for appointment to the post: 

Provided that the Central Governmenf may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under thp personal law 
applicable to such person and ihe other party to the marriage 
and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule, 

5. Power to relax.—^Where tho Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, and for rcason.s to be recorded in writing, and in 
consultation with Union Public Service Commission relax any 
of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or 
categoiy of persons. 

6. Saving,—Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
rclaxtion of age limit and other concessions required to be 
provided for candidates belonging to the Scheduled Castes, 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons 
in accordance with the orders issued by tho Cenrtal Govern¬ 
ment from time to time in ibis regard.

(Department of Company Affairs) 

New Delhi, the 2nd March, 1990 

G.S.R. 177.—In excicise of thepowers conferred by the 
proviso to the sub-section (1) of Section 594 of the Com¬ 
panies Act, 1956 (1 of 1956) read with the Government 
of India, Department of Company Affairs, Notification No, 


Ci.S.R. 44,T(E) dated Ihe t8th October, 1972 and in partial 
modification of the Notification of the Government of 
India, Ministry of Finance (Depratment of Company Law 
Administration) No. S.R.O. 3216 dated the 4tli October, 
1957 (hereafter referred to a,s the Notification) the Com¬ 
pany Law Board hereby directs that in care of M|s Montedi¬ 
son S.p.A. (hereinafter referred to as the company brintr a 
foreign company the requirements of clause (a) of sub¬ 
section 1] of the said stetion 594 as modified in their appli¬ 
cation to a foreign company by the Notification shall apply 
subject to the following further exceptions and modifications 
namely;— 

It shall be deemed to be sufficient compliance with the 
provision of clause (a) of sub-section (1) of the said sec¬ 
tion 594, if in respect of the financial year ended on 
31-12-89 the company in respect of its Indian Business ac¬ 
counts submits to the appropirate Registrars of Companies In 
India in triplicate;— 

(i) A statement of receipts and payments made by Ihe 
Indian Branch, cerifled by (1) a person authorised 
to accept service of process In India under clause 

(d) of sub-section (1) of Section 592 of the Act 
and (2) a Chartered Accountant practising in 
India. 

(ii) A statement of the company’s assets and liabilities 
in India certified in the manner as indiacted m 
item (i) above; and 

(iii) A certificate duly signed by person as indicated in 
item (!) above that the company did not carry on 
any business in India durlnj? the year ended on 
31-12-89.

the 20th August. 1989.

ceived within the ofoersaid period;

section 28 of the Indian Boilers Act, 1W3 (5 of 1923), tEe 
Central Boilers Board hereby makes the following regula¬ 
tions further to amend the Indian Boiler Regulations, 
1950.

(Amendment) Regulations, 1990.

publication in tbe Official Gazette,

G.S.R. 179,—Whereas certain draft regulations, further 
ko amend tho Indian Boiler RegulatTons, 1950, were pnh- 
lishtd as requiredc by sub-section (1) of scctlou 31 of the 
Indian Boilers Act, 1923 (5 of 1923) at page 2917 of the 
Gr.zette of India, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (1). dated 
the 25th November, 1989 under the notilicatiou of the 
Government of India in the Ministry of Industry (Dep'mt- 
ment of fndastrial DevclopnieiU) (Central Boilers Board) 
No. GSR 874 daled the 3rd November, 1989 inviting objec¬ 
tions and suggestions from all persons likely to be affected 
thereby till tlie I4lh January, 1990, 

And whcrea.s the said Gazette -was made available to the 
public on the 1st December, 1989. 

And whereas no abjections or suggestions have been 
received; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 28 of the Indian Boilers Act, 1923 (5 of 1923), tho 
Central Boilers Board hereby makes the following regula¬ 
tions further to amend the Indian Boiler Regulations, 

1950. 

1- (1) TTiese reguiations may he called the Indian Boiler 
(Second Amendment) Regiiltaions, 1990. 

(2) They shall come Into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette.

regulation 4, the words “and list^ in Apuendix G” 
anT “listed In Appendix M” shall be omitted. 

fii) in Form TIT-C, in the note, the words ‘'listed in 
Appendix K” shall be omitted. 

(iii) Appendices C, G, T, K and M shall be deleted.

MINISTRY OF ENVIRONMENT AND FORESTS 
(Department of Environment, Foic.sts and Wildlifej 
NATIONAL MUSEUM OF NATURAL HISTORY 
New Delhi, the 26th February, 1990 

O.S.R. 1H2.—^In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to artiele 309 of the Constitution and in supersession 
of the Natural Flistory Museum, Deparluieut of Science anti 
Technology (Group ‘C’ posts) Recruitment Rules, 1975, in so 
far as they relate to the post of Assistant Librarian, except 
as respects things done or omitted to be done betoro such 
supersession, the President hereby makes the following rufe 
regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Assistant 
Librarian in the National Museum of Natural History, Minis¬ 
try of Environment and Forests, namely 

1. Short title and commencement.—(1) These rules may be 
culled the Ministry of Environment and Forests, National 
Museum of Natural History (Assistant Librarian) Recruitment 
Rules, 1989. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of po.sl, classification and scale of pay .-—^The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scale of 
pay attached therefo, shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 
of ll»e Schedule annexed to (hese rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications, 
etc.—'The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and 


other matters relating to tlie said po.st shall bo us specified 
in columns 5 to 14 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualification.—No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living ; or 

(b) Vv'ho, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracled a marriage with (yjy person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to Ihe said post: 

Provided tliat the Central Government may, if salisficd that 
such marriage is permissible under the personnel law appli¬ 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt arty person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax.—Where the Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may. 
by order and for reasons to be recorded in writing, lelax any 
of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or 
category of persons. 

6 . Saving.—-Nothing in tlicse rules shall affect reservations, 
iclax.ation of age limit and other concessions required to be 
'■.rovided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes, tTit 
Rx-scrvicemcn and other special categories of persons in 
accordance wiih the orders issued by (he Central Govemment 
from time to time in this regard.

G.S.R. 183.—In exercise of the powers confeneil by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution the President heic- 
by makes the following rules legulating the method of re¬ 
cruitment to the post of Senior Radiogiapher (Supervisor) 
in the Safdarang Hospital, New Delhi, namelir : —

be called the Sfcnior Radiographer (Supervisor), Group ‘B' 
Non-Gazetted Reciuitment Rules, 1990. 

(2) They shall eorne into force on tlif date of their 
publication in the Ofllcial Gazette. 

2. Number of post, elassilication and scale of the pa)'-— 

The number of the said post, its classffictaion and the scale 

of pay attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 

to 4 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of rccruitnient, age limit, qualification etc.— 

The method of recruitment, age Hmit, qualifications and 

other matters relating to the said post shall be as specified 
in columns 5 to 14 of the said Schedule, 


4. Disqualification.—No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage 
witli a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who. having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post. 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marri- 
oge and that there arc other grounds for so doing, exempt 
any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax.—Where the Central Government i.s 
of the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, 
it may. by order and for reasons to be recorded to rvriling, 
relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to 
any class or category of persons. 

6 . Saving.—Nothing in these rules shall affect reserva¬ 
tions, relaxation of age limit and other concessions required 
to be provided for the Scheduled Caste, the Scheduled 
Tribes and other special category of persons in accordance 
with the orders issued by the Central Government from 
time to time in this regard.
